# HP5 PLUS 135 black-and-white silver negative film: optical exposure and manual processing

## 1. Scope and Applicability

This physical-product profile covers one complete non-cinematographic black-and-white silver-image negative on HP5 PLUS 135 acetate-base film, after actual optical exposure, development, stopping, nonhardening fixation, washing, final wetting rinse and drying. The output is first-quality-released net film strip, including its retained support, emulsion/image and actual conditioning moisture, with removable cassette, spool, sleeves and transport packaging excluded. Image count and subject do not define another manufacturing identity.

The selected foreground receives complete purchased unexposed HP5 PLUS 135 film and separately identified whole liquid concentrates. Actual camera exposure is followed by light-tight loading, one-shot ILFOTEC DD-X manual spiral development, ILFOSTOP stop bath, RAPID FIXER, actual film washing, ILFOTOL final rinse and ambient hanging drying in a clean area, then inspection and unique first release. Temperature conditioning and ventilation are measured where performed. Manufacturer originals establish this compatible technical route; they are not one contemporary factory/order record. Film coating, chemical manufacture, camera manufacture and photographic creative services remain actual upstream/use links, not invented onsite steps. Sources: ilford-hp5-2018; ilford-ddx-2019; ilford-first-film.

Exclude unexposed film or exposed undeveloped latent images as the reference output, paper positive prints, digital images, chemical products, cameras, cinematographic film, colour C41/E6, reversal, 120/sheet-format variants and medical/industrial radiation film. No bleach, toner, hardener, emulsion coating, boiler or onsite silver-recovery plant is inferred. Different actual routes require an explicit complete extension; their real burdens and abnormal releases cannot be deleted. Supplier tables are guidance for their stated product/process, not universal development settings, dilution, silver/gelatin fraction, thickness, density, energy, yield, lifetime or emission defaults.

Conventional black-and-white silver-gelatin chemistry forms a latent image on exposure, reduces exposed silver halide to image silver during development, and transfers remaining halide through fixing complexes into actual baths/washes. CCI Notes16/6 supports these qualitative distinctions only. Its maximum-permanence discussion and film/fibre-paper table include hardening/two-bath/toning alternatives and historic settings; those are not added to this HP5-specific manufacturer route and establish no archival-life claim. Source: cci-photo-processing-2007.

The native Product family positively identifies exposed and developed photographic plates/film other than cinematographic film, as a finished manufactured production mix at plant. This profile narrows it to complete HP5 PLUS135 acetate-base silver-image negatives after fixation, washing and drying. It does not identify an unexposed precursor, latent image, paper print, camera, digital file or mixture of products. Native Mass/kg measures full net film, not silver alone or a presumed per-roll weight. Manufacturer construction and actual supplier/provider compatibility remain separately declared.

Declare every required qualifier in full dataset metadata or equivalent reference context; absence leaves that data-package reference incomplete.

Include retained acetate support, image/emulsion and moisture once; exclude removable packing, rejected footage and removed destructive samples. Closing first-released FG is already included in Q. |

Counts/length/area need independently measured same-lot net unit mass or areal mass with sampling and uncertainty; record actual perforations/cuts. Do not infer kg from camera count, nominal frame count, envelope geometry, substrate thickness or a database secondary coefficient. A measured per-item mass is a collection result, never a guessed value. |
| `whole_solution` | concentrates and waste liquids | Mass | kg | Use whole as-supplied wet formulation mass once. Its water/active ingredient assays are non-additive subledgers. Independently measure actual added dilution water and any separately purchased adjustment chemical. Volume-to-mass needs the specific solution density at the measured state; water density is not another bath density. |

Integrate genuine rates over time; already integrated liquid/gas volumes receive no extra time multiplier. Match concentration to actual dry/wet gas volume, temperature/pressure and sampling state. |
| `gross_and_burden` | all physical gates | native property | kg; MJ | Keep full physical gross V and its native unit separate from attributed burden A and historical stock vectors. Each result references the one section8 normalization on Q or original Qc. H is an observation interval, not another divisor. Internal transfers cancel only as matched physical endpoints. |

P is new separately acquired article input; I is article arriving with actual contents once, including supplier-included articles whose embodied burdens are already in the supplier aggregate; ER is actual empty article returned IN from customer/pool. S is ALL filled gross outward article shipments including actual filled returns once; EO is separate empty article outward; W is one whole wet discarded article. These legs are mutually exclusive. Internal attach/detach/reuse cancels by matched IDs. Whole assembly versus measured disjoint parts is chosen once. No automatic coproduct, purchase per reuse, credit or reference film mass. |  |
| wet_process_gate | wet_process_gate | Manufacturing total wet mass: O_pre+R_full+F_nonwater+H2O_in+OTHER_IN-X_OTHER_IN = C_pre+T_release+E_full+J_nonwater_out+H2O_out+OTHER_OUT-X_OTHER_OUT. R_full and E_full are disjoint whole physical receipts/exits; do not add their contained water/components again. F_nonwater and J_nonwater_out are actual separate nonwater crossings, not invented reaction corrections; chemical conversion does not conserve each reacting species. FG total wet mass: O_FG+T_release+R_return+J_in+H2O_in+OTHER_IN-X_OTHER_IN = C_FG+D_out+E_FG+J_out+H2O_out+OTHER_OUT-X_OTHER_OUT. T_release is an identical measured internal handoff and cancels once between gates. Q0 is its locked reference-event account, not an additional physical exit. Distinguish ambient through-moisture from net uptake/release, actual reaction water from carrier water and evaporation, and actual externally supplied water from thermal-bath circulation. Independent water/species measurements are required; a single residual cannot estimate two unknowns. For each asset k: opening_k+new_k+returns_in_k+real_gains_k = closing_k+outward_k+whole_retired_k+real_losses_k, with measured conditioning/corrosion/contamination changes explicit. Whole retirement retains any contamination and one receiver. Reusable cotton glove, reusable chamois and film squeegee are separately surveyed; cellulose wipe is not their proxy. Keep full wash/cleaning water, detergent if actually used, sample residues and whole wet retirement, one actual complete provider interface and nonoverlap with embedded manufacturer assets. Optional actual hairdryer or other equipment needs its own complete extension; ambient drying does not authorise unmetered forced drying. | ilford-first-film; kodak-module3 |
| residual_method_1330a | residual_method_1330a | Fixer film-clearing activity tests and visible negative inspection are real manufacturer procedures but are not quantitative washing-residual assays. For quantitative residual thiosulfate use the separately complete historical Kodak1330A methylene-blue branch with its own extractant, potassium-borohydride/NaOH, acetone, acid ferric-SULFATE and NND-sulfate/Florisil reagent preparations and standardized thiosulfate calibration; do not replace its ferric sulfate with later ULM0004 ferric chloride. Its scope explicitly includes processed film containing silver; immediate analysis and minimum-density selection are binding because residual thiosulfate changes with time/image silver. Record actual low-density area, whole sample net emulsion-bearing area excluding holes, layer/backing state, sampling coverage, time from processing and sample disposition; 16mm length is an example, not a135 conversion. Reduced sample size is explicitly permitted; use actual area once. Extract/remove sample before colour development, maintain actual addition order/timing, reagent-age and distilled-water/copper suitability; use actual calibrated dropping/delivery devices and own instrument curve, reagent blank and standards. For high levels apply the complete AppendixII aliquot/dilution branch and actual dilution factors. Calculate measured analyte mass in the full extract divided by actual sampled area once; calibration mass is S2O3 ion, not automatically anhydrous or pentahydrate sodium-salt mass. No printed curve, recipe, acceptance limit, reported sensitivity or source numerical correction becomes a default. AppendixIV copper and expired/low-assay borohydride effects must be considered; actual sample recovery, repeats, blanks and representativeness qualify the study. Availability of this full method is distinct from unperformed actual lot QA and establishes no archival-life claim. | kodak-1330a |
| method_countercontexts | method_countercontexts | Keep the later ULM0004/1 complete procedure and its actual limitations: Module4 lists ferric chloride hexahydrate but supplies no liquid-reagent preparation required by the volumetric addition; its at-least1inch sheet-edge wording and calibration-area convention are not silently rewritten for35mm. This is a gap of that branch, not proof that all residual-film methods are absent. NIST1963 supplies real35mm perforated-film extraction and gelatin-controlled turbidimetry but explicitly residual silver also adds turbidity interpreted as thiosulfate; low-level nonlinearity, image/gelatin/time/temperature effects and its ASA1958 standard-preparation crossreference remain. It is retained as independent counterevidence, not selected quantitative HP5 assay or universal1µg/in² correction. Neither a clearing test nor a paper sulfide spot test measures total washing residuals. Keep each quantitative claim limited to actual validated matrix, sample preparation/selectivity/recovery and detection. Unknown necessary procedure blocks that affected claim; future SOP or finite software pass does not supply it. Ordinary calibration/actual lot testing not yet performed is a study qualification gap, not proof that a documented complete method does not exist. | kodak-module3; kodak-module4; nist-residual |
| laboratory_interfaces | laboratory_interfaces | Every actually performed assay includes actual destructive film withdrawal, each separately supplied reagent or one real whole premix, water quality production, weighing/mixing/heating/cooling/stirring/instrument use, standards/blanks/repeats, vessel cleaning, retained samples and all actual gases/liquids/solids. Whole external analytical service may replace a fully covered actual laboratory producer only when that provider really performs the whole named method with matched sample-send/receive quantities and all upstream/reagent/utility/waste burdens; it cannot hide actual onsite work. No sample mass is both retained product and exported waste at the same gate. Quantitative fixer/bath silver is total elemental concentration in the stated matrix, not freeAg(I); use a complete compatible procedure with actual preparation/calibration/interference/recovery. No spent-fixer silver concentration or universal recovery rate is borrowed from another film process. TotalAg and full wet liquid mass close different ledgers and must not solve each other by residual fitting; any freeAg(I) environmental claim requires independent speciation. | kodak-1330a; kodak-module3; ilcd-detailed-2010 |

It measures Ag-equivalent concentration of the actual original bath aliquot; it does not establish freeAg(I), original complex proportions, film Ag or refractory/sludge whole-phase digestion. EDTA does not remove zinc interference; prevent acidic thiosulfate decomposition and Ag2S coagulation, and retain actual sample-volume/added-thiosulfate response conditions. Standardize by actual known Ag-equivalent response and divide by the original sample aliquot, not the enlarged assay mixture. Use the historical AR714 actual1M thiosulfate matrix in its independent complete sulfide standardization, corroborating1208E1M, while explicitly retaining laterModule4p35 wording1N as a source conflict; N and M are not synonyms. Source1209D independently covers multiple thiosulfate salt matrices but its0.0926/0.00926 caption and ferricyanideM/N inconsistencies remain; it is not a silent numerical substitute. These real methods do not provide site concentrations, recovery factors, all-phase digestion or discharge permission. If actual matrix/speciation needs exceed their established scope, that affected quantity remains unresolved; do not fabricate it from wet mass or a provider label. | kodak-module3; kodak-module4; kodak-1330a |


## 6. Process Inventory Structure

### Process Map

| process_id | process_name | inclusion | inclusion_condition | role | quantitative_reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| negative_production | Optical exposure through first dry-negative release | required | Complete selected manual route with actual support operations | foreground | per 1 kg reference flow |
| fg_service | Observed original-cohort storage handling and services | conditional | All actually performed services and physical events in H, including held goods | foreground | per 1 kg reference flow |
| packaging_pool | Complete named-article packaging custody pools | conditional | Every actual supplier and delivery package with disjoint acquisition scope | foreground | per 1 kg reference flow |

Each card is one atomic exchange. Conditional means supported physical applicability, not an assumed positive quantity or automatic zero. Read complete process/section conditions with each card. Amount and aggregation labels refer to the single section8 result; they perform no arithmetic themselves. Physical gross quantities and separately attributed burden views remain distinct.

### Process: Optical exposure through first dry-negative release (`negative_production`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Complete purchased unexposed HP5 PLUS135 film (`unexposed_film`)

One complete supplied sensitized film including support and coating; net media measured independently from cassette/spool/packaging. No extra purchase of the embedded gelatin, silver salts or acetate. Use supplier lot and upstream converting/loading coverage; an integrated-coating-only intermediate or an exposure-only transfer does not establish this complete supplied-film interface.

#### Outputs

##### Product flows

###### Unique first released complete negative film (`first_negative_release`)

The native Product family positively identifies exposed and developed photographic plates/film other than cinematographic film, as a finished manufactured production mix at plant. This profile narrows it to complete HP5 PLUS135 acetate-base silver-image negatives after fixation, washing and drying. It does not identify an unexposed precursor, latent image, paper print, camera, digital file or mixture of products. Native Mass/kg measures full net film, not silver alone or a presumed per-roll weight. Manufacturer construction and actual supplier/provider compatibility remain separately declared.

- Selected flow: Photographic plates and film, exposed and developed, other than cinematographic film `1429f228-90cb-4591-ac87-55d195b4426e`

## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

| rule_id | applies_to | rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allocation_scope` | attributional multifunctional processes | Use this hierarchy only for attributional cases requiring allocation. First assign dedicated inputs/operations directly and investigate actual or virtual subdivision by batch, bath, equipment and function. Do not use manufacturer instructions as LCA allocation evidence. | ilcd-detailed-2010 |
| `determining_causality` | each remaining shared exchange | Establish the physical relationship determining each exchange separately, using actual meters/chemistry/processing observations. Bath turnover, film area, exposure time, thermal capacity or occupancy are candidate drivers only when demonstrated to determine that exchange. One convenient mass or time share is not automatically valid for all electricity, chemicals, silver and space services. | ilcd-detailed-2010 |
| `residual_economic` | demonstrably unresolved residual burdens | Only residual burdens without a determining physical relationship use justified contemporaneous market values at the same common-process exit and actual state/quality/quantity. Remove downstream finishing, packing, transport and service value where necessary to recover that gate value; disclose estimation and sensitivity. Do not freely choose physical versus economic, fixed shares, or creative-image sales value for already separable processing. | ilcd-detailed-2010 |
| `vector_not_mass` | physical ledgers and burdens | Allocation divides burden vectors, not physical outgoing wet mass. Preserve full gross quantities and stock history, conserve every exchange vector across assigned and unassigned functions, and keep cohort services separate. If software cannot express custody separately from burden attribution, construct explicit measured stock/service units rather than duplicate providers or shrink physical flows. | ilcd-detailed-2010 |
| `silver_no_credit` | silver-bearing waste and any recovery | Sale or transfer of silver-bearing liquid, sludge or waste film does not itself move manufacturing burdens to the receiver or earn avoided-silver credit. Retain full waste state, actual treatment and historical burdens. Any real recovered product and separate recovery process require their actual inventory and a separately justified allocation boundary; no assumed recovery yield. | ilcd-detailed-2010; ilford-rapid-2010 |

## 8. Foreground Data Collection, Calculation, and Quality Rules

Protocols collect raw unnormalized totals, states and inherited vectors. aggregation_rule declares the common final reference basis only; all conversions, attribution and the one division occur in the calculation rules. Keep original Qc and H for post-release services, including work on held goods.

### Calculation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| native_units | raw records | Convert raw measurements before attribution: kWh × 3.6 = MJ; actual count × independently measured same-lot net item mass = kg; actual area × independently measured same-state areal mass = kg; liquid volume × independently measured solution density at recorded temperature = kg. Integrate a rate once; a cumulative volume is already integrated. Keep original units and uncertainty. | raw_records; independent_conversion_measurements | native_numerator |  |
| production_vectors | manufacturing burden vectors | For each exchange i on a gross-receipt basis: A_prod_i = B_openRaw_i + B_openWIP_i + B_openNonWIP_i + B_new_i - B_closeRaw_i - B_closeWIP_i - B_closeNonWIP_i, after dedicated/virtual subdivision and justified residual attribution. All sets are disjoint and all components retain their native unit. B_new includes actual failed/zero-output/start-stop/idle/cleanup work. First-released FG is never a manufacturing Bclose term. Already-net consumption uses its documented equivalent bridge without repeating stock correction. Preserve unassigned burdens if no causal positive-Q cohort exists. | native_numerator; cp_stock_vectors; allocation_evidence | A_prod_i | ilcd-detailed-2010 |
| service_vectors | fg_service and packaging_pool | Retain full historical cohort vectors and physical opening/closing FG/package quantities. A_service_i is only actual new attributable service burden observed in H, including failed work, rewash and cleaning, linked to original Qc; it is not the full historic manufacture purchased again. Package acquisition/use/disposal history and unassigned pool vectors reconcile independently. Gross physical V is not multiplied by an economic or burden share. | cp_fg; cp_packaging; stock_vectors; allocation_evidence | A_service_i | ilcd-detailed-2010 |
| single_normalization | all inventory rows | Modeled result_i,c = A_i,c / original positive Q0,c once, after actual conversion and nonoverlapping attribution. Record physical observation V/Qt and actual provider quantity Ut once; if reported Qt/Q_analysis is explicitly physical and cannot be the full shared modeled numerator per cohort. Actual same-gate reference first_negative_release is exactly1kg. Production A_prod and newly observed services A_service remain separate; H is no divisor. Zero/unknown Q0 retains raw quantities and unresolved attribution. | A_i,c; original_Q0,c; V; Qt; Ut; Q_analysis | result_i,c |  |
| physical_reconciliation | all physical gates | At each gate reconcile opening stocks plus full actual receipts/uptakes against closing stocks plus full actual outputs/releases, with whole-wet or mutually exclusive component accounting. Internal bath/film/package handoffs cancel only with matched IDs and equal quantities. Keep total mass, H2O and relevant element ledgers independent of species/reaction equations; no guessed correction flow, dual-unknown residual fit, or stock term removed merely because a row exists. | cp_materials; cp_baths; cp_water; cp_waste; cp_species; cp_release; cp_stock_vectors; cp_fg; cp_packaging | physical_ledgers_and_residuals |  |

### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| quality_identity | all inventory rows | Check full native type, baseName, synonyms/comments, CAS, grade/route/form/geography/medium and every property against actual supply/terminal. Reference film identity is distinct from provider completeness. Exact names and chemical specificity remain even when auxiliary UUID is unresolved. | Full native objects and exact chains; actual supplier and receiver records |
| quality_window | production and services | Cover all actual start-stop/idle/failed/zero-output/rework/cleanup/sampling events and stock/vector continuity, with original Qc/H for actual later services. No complete-site claim from a partial observation or an acceptance label. | Raw records and event/stock reconciliation |
| quality_species | mass water silver and reactions | Independent measurements with detection limits and uncertainty support full wet mass, actual component/element balances and separate phase/species assignments. Record analytical total Ag/COD/VOC as indicators; no forced chemical identity or invented reaction/evaporation closure. | Sampling plans; laboratory methods; moisture/density and outlet evidence |
| quality_route | negative terminal and process settings | Use batch-compatible actual manufacturer controls and measured film quality. The HP5 characteristic curve using HC, DELTA100 beginner example, tray/rotary alternatives, paper permanence sequences and future-dated SDS revision field do not supply default HP5 DD-X values or review approval. | Complete source context; actual control/acceptance records |
| quality_sources | source use and modelling boundaries | Retain source-specific product/process/state/use compatibility and distinguish originals, mirrors, reuse and extraction dates. Manufacturer sources do not prove LCA allocation; ILCD allocation does not prove photochemical applicability. Foreground missing quantities/providers are explicit collection gaps, not invented measurements. | Source/context lineage and foreground gap register |

## 9. Validation Rules

| rule_id | applies_to | rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `validate_terminal` | first_negative_release | Exactly one linked Product reference output is the fully developed, fixed, washed and dry non-cine negative. Native Mass/kg, net same-state measurement, EN/ZH first-release gate and complete qualifiers agree. No latent precursor or returned old strip becomes new Q. | ilford-hp5-2018; ilford-first-film |
| `validate_atomic` | all inventory rows | Each card is one specific physical/chemical exchange with correct type/property/unit/medium, complete native restrictions and actual supply/receiver. Every real extra flow is added separately. A blank auxiliary UUID neither authorizes a collection label nor erases an actual exchange. |  |
| `validate_once` | collection and calculation | Raw protocols collect unnormalized measurements. Unit/rate conversion and genuine attribution precede single_normalization exactly once. Audit all table cells, ten card fields, complete descriptions, qualifier elements, formula Inputs/Output, source fields/lists and quality requirements in both projections. |  |
| `validate_stocks` | manufacturing FG and packaging | Verify disjoint raw/WIP/non-WIP vectors and no manufacturing Bclose deduction of already released FG. Check all gross dispatch/return events, Qc/H service increments including held goods, and complete package pool equations. Combined handoffs cancel without deleting external flows or stocks. |  |
| `validate_waste_species` | waste and environmental outlets | Require actual full wet transfers and one provider; mixed origins are non-additive. Verify actual silver forms and environmental media; total silver/COD/VOC is not substituted for Ag(I), SO2 or another molecule. Preserve unauthorized/failed-work releases and unresolved residuals. |  |
| `validate_allocation` | burden vectors | Verify dedicated/subdivision evidence, exchange-specific determining physical causality and only genuinely residual same-gate/state market value; all assigned/unassigned vectors reconcile. No fixed share, automatic silver credit or physical mass scaled by allocation. | ilcd-detailed-2010 |
| `validate_completeness` | complete foreground package | Unknown required terminal identity, conversion, method relation, reaction/species split, actual operation, provider or measurement remains explicitly incomplete. Ordinary future-site measurements and auxiliary UUID gaps remain requirements; mechanical checks alone do not establish scientific/translation approval or publication. |  |

### Complete retained scope, native admission and collection preambles

The following complete retained paragraphs remain binding with Q/Qc explicitly interpreted as original Q0 above. They do not replace the updated complete ledgers or reopen a retired identity.

This physical-product profile covers one complete non-cinematographic black-and-white silver-image negative on HP5 PLUS 135 acetate-base film, after actual optical exposure, development, stopping, nonhardening fixation, washing, final wetting rinse and drying. The output is first-quality-released net film strip, including its retained support, emulsion/image and actual conditioning moisture, with removable cassette, spool, sleeves and transport packaging excluded. Image count and subject do not define another manufacturing identity.

The selected foreground receives complete purchased unexposed HP5 PLUS 135 film and separately identified whole liquid concentrates. Actual camera exposure is followed by light-tight loading, one-shot ILFOTEC DD-X manual spiral development, ILFOSTOP stop bath, RAPID FIXER, actual film washing, ILFOTOL final rinse and ambient hanging drying in a clean area, then inspection and unique first release. Temperature conditioning and ventilation are measured where performed. Manufacturer originals establish this compatible technical route; they are not one contemporary factory/order record. Film coating, chemical manufacture, camera manufacture and photographic creative services remain actual upstream/use links, not invented onsite steps. Sources: ilford-hp5-2018; ilford-ddx-2019; ilford-first-film.

Exclude unexposed film or exposed undeveloped latent images as the reference output, paper positive prints, digital images, chemical products, cameras, cinematographic film, colour C41/E6, reversal, 120/sheet-format variants and medical/industrial radiation film. No bleach, toner, hardener, emulsion coating, boiler or onsite silver-recovery plant is inferred. Different actual routes require an explicit complete extension; their real burdens and abnormal releases cannot be deleted. Supplier tables are guidance for their stated product/process, not universal development settings, dilution, silver/gelatin fraction, thickness, density, energy, yield, lifetime or emission defaults.

Conventional black-and-white silver-gelatin chemistry forms a latent image on exposure, reduces exposed silver halide to image silver during development, and transfers remaining halide through fixing complexes into actual baths/washes. CCI Notes16/6 supports these qualitative distinctions only. Its maximum-permanence discussion and film/fibre-paper table include hardening/two-bath/toning alternatives and historic settings; those are not added to this HP5-specific manufacturer route and establish no archival-life claim. Source: cci-photo-processing-2007.

The native Product family positively identifies exposed and developed photographic plates/film other than cinematographic film, as a finished manufactured production mix at plant. This profile narrows it to complete HP5 PLUS135 acetate-base silver-image negatives after fixation, washing and drying. It does not identify an unexposed precursor, latent image, paper print, camera, digital file or mixture of products. Native Mass/kg measures full net film, not silver alone or a presumed per-roll weight. Manufacturer construction and actual supplier/provider compatibility remain separately declared.

Declare every required qualifier in full dataset metadata or equivalent reference context; absence leaves that data-package reference incomplete.

Each card is one atomic exchange. Conditional means supported physical applicability, not an assumed positive quantity or automatic zero. Read complete process/section conditions with each card. Amount and aggregation labels refer to the single section8 result; they perform no arithmetic themselves. Physical gross quantities and separately attributed burden views remain distinct.

Protocols collect raw unnormalized totals, states and inherited vectors. aggregation_rule declares the common final reference basis only; all conversions, attribution and the one division occur in the calculation rules. Keep original Qc and H for post-release services, including work on held goods.

#### Outputs

##### Product flows

###### Unique first released complete negative film (`first_negative_release`)

The native Product family positively identifies exposed and developed photographic plates/film other than cinematographic film, as a finished manufactured production mix at plant. This profile narrows it to complete HP5 PLUS135 acetate-base silver-image negatives after fixation, washing and drying. It does not identify an unexposed precursor, latent image, paper print, camera, digital file or mixture of products. Native Mass/kg measures full net film, not silver alone or a presumed per-roll weight. Manufacturer construction and actual supplier/provider compatibility remain separately declared.

- Selected flow: Photographic plates and film, exposed and developed, other than cinematographic film `1429f228-90cb-4591-ac87-55d195b4426e`
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66`; Units of mass `93a60a57-a4c8-11da-a746-0800200c9a66` / kg
- Amount rule: 1 kg
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per 1 kg reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_release`
- Sources: `ilford-hp5-2018`; `ilford-first-film`

###### Net water vapour to outdoor air (`vapour_air`)

Independent humidity/film/bath/waste/stock observations support net evaporation; ambient through-moisture and reaction water are separate. Conditional positive quantity requires actual species-specific environmental crossing and compatible measurements; constituent/SDS presence does not prove release. ## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | secondary_dataset; background_dataset only after actual compatible upstream and treatment links are complete |
| downstream_use | Physical manufacturing of declared HP5 PLUS135 dry silver negative with separately linked observed original-cohort services |
| allowed_use | Same-route comparisons with matched net film state, first-release gate, image quality/function and disclosed capture/upstream/service coverage |
| excluded_use | Whole38942 coverage; cine/colour/reversal/radiography/paper/digital substitutes; creative-value or archival-life equivalence per kg; complete LCI claims with unresolved actual inputs/outlets; automatic recovery credits |
| required_metadata | All reference qualifiers; exact physical gate and native units; supplier/media/chemical identities; original Qc/H; actual stock and inherited vectors; package pools; allocation; receiver/transport/treatment |
| required_quality_disclosure | Raw measurement and source coverage, uncertainty/detection, all failed/zero-output work, unassigned vectors, ordinary UUID/provider gaps, true method holds and scientific/translation state |
| update_trigger | Changed film format/support/emulsion, exposure or bath route, formulation, drying/release state, utility or treatment boundary, native property, packaging pool or attribution rule |

## 11. Data Sources

| Source id | Type | Reference | Used for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| unsd-cpc3-2025 | official_guidance | UNSD CPC Version3.0 Structure, 30 June2025, subclasses38941/38942/38950/48341/48342 and838; https://unstats.un.org/unsd/classifications/Econ/Download/In%20Text/CPC_Ver_3.0_Structure_30Jun2025.csv | Classification terminal distinction only; no manufacturing coefficient or accepted mapping claim |
| ilford-hp5-2018 | handbook | HARMAN HP5 PLUS Technical Information, November2018, complete6pages; pp1–3 format/exposure, pp5–6 processing/drying/storage; https://www.ilfordphoto.com/amfile/file/download/file/1903/product/693/ ; complete manufacturer PDF also at https://fotofilmfabriek.nl/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/HP5-technical-data-sheet-Nov.-18.pdf | 135 acetate still-film identity, complete negative route and supply state; other formats and HC curve are countercontexts; no numeric defaults |
| ilford-ddx-2019 | handbook | HARMAN ILFOTEC DD-X Technical Information, August2019, complete8pages; pp1–3 mixing/manual route, pp6–8 reuse alternatives and stop/fix/wash/dry; https://www.ilfordphoto.com/wp/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/ILFOTEC-DDX-AUG19.pdf | Whole liquid concentrate and selected one-shot spiral route, bath/oxidation context and actual quantity/control requirements; not a universal recipe |
| ilford-sundry-2017 | handbook | HARMAN Sundry Chemicals for Processing Black and White Films and Papers, December2017, complete4pages; pp2–3 ILFOSTOP/ILFOTOL, p4 adjacent WASHAID/FB paper sequences; https://www.ilfordphoto.com/amfile/file/download/file/1865/product/672/ ; manufacturer-original mirror https://www.fomei.com/editor/image/eshop_products_files/5630/Pomocne_lazne_technical%20data%20sheet.pdf | Specific stop and wetting whole formulations, manual-film use, measured control and excluded machine/paper alternatives; no dilution/density/life default |
| ilford-rapid-2010 | handbook | HARMAN RAPID FIXER Technical Information, July2010, complete5pages; pp1–4 mixture, film/paper washing, activity and clearing tests; https://www.ilfordphoto.com/amfile/file/download/file/1833/product/711/ ; manufacturer-original mirror https://assets.website-files.com/5ba2689b2e7121210bfa9af3/5c0ecc53472033a2b1b17efb_tech%20specs%20ilford%20rapid%20fixer.pdf | Nonhardening ammonium-thiosulphate whole fixer, actual bath state/clearing and silver-bearing waste; paper thresholds/recovery rates not transferred |
| ilford-first-film | handbook | ILFORD Processing Your First Black & White Film, complete11pages, March2017 footer with October2003 legacy header; pp8–11 actual loading/processing/drying/examination/storage; https://www.ilfordphoto.com/wp/wp-content/uploads/2017/04/Processing-your-first-black-and-white-film.pdf | Complete small-tank sequence and actual leader/negative/storage interfaces. DELTA100 timed example and historical drain advice are not HP5 defaults or discharge permission |

| ilford-fixer-sds-2024 | handbook | ILFORD RAPID FIXER Australian SDS, issue18July2024 revision1, complete6pages, pp2/4 mixture and handling/disposal; printed revision-date field17July2027 retained without future-edition claim; https://www.ilfordphoto.com/wp/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/AUS-Rapid-Fixer.pdf | Whole ammonium-thiosulphate/bisulphite/acetic-acid formulation interface; actual waste/species screening only, no complete composition or release/recovery default |
| ilcd-detailed-2010 | official_guidance | European Commission JRC, ILCD Handbook General guide for Life Cycle Assessment Detailed guidance, first edition March2010, §§7.9.2–7.9.3 printed255–272/PDF275–292 with adjacent examples; https://eplca.jrc.ec.europa.eu/uploads/ILCD-Handbook-General-guide-for-LCA-DETAILED-GUIDANCE-12March2010-ISBN-fin-v1.0-EN.pdf | Attributional subdivision/virtual subdivision, exchange-specific determining physical causality and residual same-gate/state market value; no photochemical recipe, QFD/fixed share or automatic recycling credit |
| cci-photo-processing-2007 | official_guidance | Canadian Conservation Institute Notes16/6, Processing Contemporary Black-and-White Photographic Films and Papers for Maximum Permanence, originally1986/revised1996 and2007; complete original page, Photographic Process/Development/Fixing/Washing and adjacent Table1; https://www.canada.ca/en/conservation-institute/services/conservation-preservation-publications/canadian-conservation-institute-notes/black-white-photographic-films-papers-permanence.html | Qualitative latent/silver image/fixing-complex distinctions and actual residual-chemical measurement need; maximum-permanence, hardener, two-bath, toning and historic numerical examples remain countercontexts, not HP5 defaults or allocation evidence |
| kodak-1330a | handbook | Kodak historical analytical collection, undated177p primary scan; method1330A PDF151–153/SP945–947 and AppendixI–IV, AR703/705/708 plus standardization crossunits; https://www.filmatlas.com/images/tinyUpload/filmAtlas/2024/03/eastman-cri-references02final.pdf | Independent complete residualthiosulfate methylene-blue/ferricsulfate method, timing/density/area/layer/dilution/calibration/copper limits; CRI manufacturing is excluded; source values not defaults |
| kodak-module3 | handbook | Kodak Processing Motion Picture Films Module3 Analytical Procedures, complete304p publisherPDF, copyright1999/PDFmetadata2002; ULM0004/1 complete4p,1209D complete4p and introduction/crossreferences; https://www.kodak.com/content/products-brochures/Film/Processing-KODAK-Motion-Picture-Films-Module-3.pdf | Full analytical procedures/countercontexts; ULMchloride preparation missing inModule4; no imported cine process/film recipe or Ag species equivalence |
| kodak-module4 | handbook | Kodak Processing Motion Picture Films Module4 Reagent Preparation Procedures, complete46p copyright1999/PDFmetadata2002; completeQC/preparation and ULM0004/1209D reagent crossunits; https://www.kodak.com/content/products-brochures/Film/Processing-KODAK-Motion-Picture-Films-Module-4.pdf | Real reagent preparation/standardization and missingchloride-definition counterevidence, source print/unit inconsistencies retained; no reagent/recipe defaults |
